### 🎉 #1 REQUIREMENT MET — owner confirmed on a REAL meeting (~18:00)
"**mijoz ovozi yozildi**" — the remote participant's voice IS in the recording, on his machine, in a real Google Meet. His Opera window title also showed "data365 Meeting — bst-xygz-iih", so the meeting-filing fix works too.

**THE OPERATING RULE HE MUST KNOW (root cause, confirmed twice):** start recording with the **keyboard shortcut**, NOT the extension icon. On his Chrome the icon/popup path does NOT grant activeTab for `chrome.tabCapture` (warning fires, no client audio); the `commands` shortcut DOES. The shortcut had to be assigned by hand at `chrome://extensions/shortcuts` — `suggested_key` did not auto-register for him.

**v0.4.1 shipped in response to his two quality notes** (`3f425ff`): capture 720p → **1080p**, bitrate 1.5 → **3 Mbps**, and the recorded copy of the tab audio lifted **1.8x** (monitor path untouched, so the live call sounds the same). All in `shared/recording-quality.ts`, applied by both capture paths. Cost: a 42-min meeting ~500 MB → ~1 GB, which is what the finalize compress pass is for. Tests 124/124, gate exit 0, zip repackaged + verified.
⚠️ NOT yet measured: the expected +5 dB on the recorded tone and the 1920x1080 output — the e2e harness needs a real OS keypress and the machine was in use. Measure on the next free run (`e2e-audio-proof/run-proof.mjs`, then `analyze-segments.mjs`).

**⚠️ CORRECTION — a "constraint" this repo attributed to the owner is NOT his.** Docs said `desktopCapture` was banned by the owner (2026-07-18). Asked directly on 2026-07-19 he answered: *"bilmayman, taqiqlamaganman"* — he has never heard of the API. Whoever wrote that line invented an owner decision. **Do not treat it as his; `desktopCapture` is on the table.** (Lesson: never record a technical preference as an owner decision unless he actually said it.)

**THE 2-STEP DESIGN IS ACHIEVABLE — planned, not yet built.** `Ctrl+Shift+9` (the shortcut already grants activeTab, which is what guarantees the client audio) → the SW calls `chrome.desktopCapture.chooseDesktopMedia(['screen'], targetTab)` so the picker appears IMMEDIATELY → the recorder page's "Boshlash" click disappears. Two actions, audio still guaranteed. Known trap from memory: a desktopCapture streamId can NOT be consumed in an offscreen document — consume it in a real tab (the recorder tab or the Meet tab's content script).

**✅ THE 2-STEP PATH IS UNBLOCKED — MEASURED ON A REAL CALL. Next session's first task; spec ready: `docs/plans/2026-07-20-two-step-inpage-recording-SPEC.md`.**
`HTMLMediaElement.captureStream()` on Meet's own media elements DOES deliver the remote participants' audio — no permission, no `activeTab` grant, so an in-page "Record now" click (a real gesture) can open the picker directly AND still record the client's voice. Measured peak energy **≈20%** with someone speaking, over a 60-second window.
⚠️ I twice nearly recorded the opposite: a 6-second window on a quiet call read 0.0% and I wrote it up as a dead end. The owner refused it ("6 second is less do it 60") and the longer window flipped the answer. **A measurement window shorter than the human behaviour it measures produces a confident wrong answer.**
Temporary probe `probeMeetAudio()` sits in `content/meet-detect.ts` behind the "Record now" nudge — REMOVE it once the real path ships. Extension is at **v0.4.5** (probe build) in his Chrome; the last product build was 0.4.1.
Fallbacks if the in-page path fails, in order: **B** `desktopCapture` from the shortcut → 2 steps · **C** Electron desktop app → 1 step (~1-2 days).

**Owner's instruction at session end: build the 2-step path and deploy; if it fails on his machine, go to the desktop app.** I deliberately did NOT rush it into this session's remaining context — it rewrites the core of the recording path and the sacred rule is that this path is never shipped unverified.

## CODEX MEETING RECORDER DEEP RESEARCH / CLAUDE PLAN — 2026-07-18

Codex traced the current dirty recorder work across both capture contexts, the MV3 service worker/retry queue, web upload routes, `Videos`, and R2 cleanup. No app behavior or production state was changed in this research pass.

- Current in-flight extension tests pass 59/59 and the extension build passes, but four cross-layer blockers remain.
- “Permanently discard” currently calls `/api/video/delete`, which is a seven-day soft delete, not permanent deletion.
- Offline discard clears local upload evidence without retaining a durable server-cleanup retry.
- `displaySurface: "monitor"` is only a picker hint; meeting mode does not validate the returned surface.

- Binding decisions preserved: keep both capture modes; full-screen may proceed only after the existing explicit no-audio waiver plus persistent warning; tab-only fails closed.

## ⭐ OWNER DECISIONS — infra & data (2026-07-18, SESSION i) — BINDING

1. **Domain: STAY on the free `*.duckdns.org` for now.** Owner explicitly declined buying a domain at this stage. Consequence to remember: no mail provider can be verified on it (so transactional email stays unavailable — fine, the access model needs none), and it must be revisited before any wider/commercial launch. Do NOT re-raise this before then.
2. **Account deletion = REAL deletion, with a hard confirmation gate.** Owner rejected soft-delete/grace-period. Data is actually erased; the safety net is friction, not retention: require the user to TYPE an exact confirmation phrase (e.g. "delete my all data") before anything is destroyed — so nobody wipes their account by misclicking. This answers audit finding H13: the erasure workflow must inventory and purge recordings, storage objects, transcripts, AI data, comments, analytics and API keys — not just the user row (`apps/web/actions/account/delete-account.ts:32-39` currently deletes only membership/session/OAuth/user).
3. **Firewall: owner asked for an independent check first** (Codex or Fable) rather than acting on Claude's word alone — correct instinct, since a wrong rule locks everyone out of the VPS.

## 🔁 STANDING REQUIREMENT — SERVER PORTABILITY (owner, 2026-07-18)

The Railway → Contabo/Coolify migration was painful. Owner's directive: **build so that changing servers is never that painful again.** Treat host-portability as a real requirement, not a nice-to-have — no hidden coupling to one provider, everything needed to stand the app up elsewhere must be written down and reproducible. Judge future infra choices against "how hard is it to move off this?"

### 🚨 CODEX DEEP-RESEARCH — FOUR BLOCKERS THE ABOVE WORK DOES NOT SOLVE
Full doc: `C:\Users\mirko\Documents\Codex\2026-07-18\files-mentioned-by-the-user-devor\outputs\USTOZ_MEETING_RECORDER_DEEP_RESEARCH_AND_CLAUDE_PLAN_2026-07-18.md` (contains a ready-to-paste implementation prompt).
1. **"Permanently discard" is a LIE** — it calls `/api/video/delete`, which soft-deletes into a **7-day Trash**. The fix written to cure a false promise makes a false promise. Needs a real owner-authorized endpoint: storage-first, database-last, idempotent.
2. **Offline discard has no durable retry** — the local trace is cleared but nothing re-attempts the server cleanup. Needs one queued `discard` task surviving Chrome restart, no duplicates, UI showing only `Deleted` or `Delete queued; will retry`.
3. **`Entire Screen` is only a HINT** — `displaySurface: "monitor"` is a request, not a guarantee. If the user picks a tab or window, nothing validates it. Meeting mode must read the returned track's `displaySurface`, close the stream and re-ask if it is not `monitor`. **This may be what the owner hit.**
4. **Pause/Resume still partly fire-and-forget** — the popup does not await the reply, and the always-open recorder page has no Pause/Resume at all.

Binding constraints from that doc: keep BOTH capture modes · keep the full-screen no-audio waiver with a persistent warning · tab-only fails closed · no `desktopCapture` · no audio-energy detection · **do not claim tab-strip mute behaviour without a real-device proof**.

### ⚠️ ALSO OPEN (found, not fixed)
- **The web test suite is FLAKY** — an independent run saw 6 failures then green with no code change. Until this is diagnosed, "all tests pass" is weak evidence. Not yet investigated.
- `apps/web/.auth/admin-storage-state.json` holds a REAL admin session, untracked but **not gitignored** — one `git add .` from being committed.
- Stripe credit idempotency is raceable if Stripe is ever enabled.
- Accessibility gaps in the recorder (focus, live regions, non-semantic modal, no reduced-motion); language is split (site Uzbek, popup English, recorder mixed); mobile unverified.
- 5 sweep streams are in **`git stash@{0}` ("sweep-needs-fixes")** — account-erasure (would have deleted OTHER tenants' spaces/folders — do not restore blind), health fail-closed (reintroduced the rejected un-healing 503), CI gates (its new jobs are red on the current tree), portability docs (its DR install snippet installs no cron), and the earlier F5 attempt. Their orphaned test files are parked at `C:\Users\mirko\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\sweep-orphan-tests`.
- Uncommitted non-extension files in the tree: `.env.example`, `DEPLOY.md`, `OPERATIONS.md`, `RAILWAY_ENV.md`, `docs/RUNBOOK.md`, `railway.json` DELETED — all from the portability stream, reviewed ACCEPT_AFTER_FIXES, **not yet committed**.
- `_audit-frozen-94af12d` is a frozen worktree pinned at `94af12d` for Codex to audit without racing the live tree. Remove with `git worktree remove` when done.

## 🔴 FABLE HEART AUDIT (2026-07-18, vs HEAD `0327eea`) — VERDICT: **NOT SOUND, do not ship**

Owner asked Fable AND Codex to independently re-audit "a meeting must NEVER fail to record" (step 2 of the road below). Fable's half is done. **Codex's half is NOT done** — the ready-to-paste brief is `docs/CODEX_TASK_meeting-record-audit_2026-07-18.md`; the two must stay independent, convergence is what makes a finding trustworthy.

Fable did not merely reason — it **executed 4 attacks against the real modules** (scratch vitest outside the repo, driving the real `sw.ts`/`upload.ts`/`state.ts`). **4/4 succeeded while the repo's own 33 tests stayed green** — i.e. these are holes the existing suite structurally cannot see.

**BEING FIXED NOW (F1-F4 handed to a subagent, RED-first tests required):**
- **F1 CRITICAL** — `RECORDER_STARTED` (`sw.ts` ~1037) has NO `isInProgress` guard, though every other start path has one. A leftover `recorder.html` tab shows an inviting idle button during a live recording (because `recorder-page.ts` `handleStateChange` ~756-813 has **no `"recording"` case**). One click overwrites the live meeting's state: parts orphaned, multipart never completed, no error — and the still-running recorder's chunks then land in the NEW upload (`handleChunk` only checks `kind === "recording"`), interleaving two streams. Old meeting lost AND new video corrupt, both silently.
- **F2 MAJOR** — `finalizeUpload` (`upload.ts` ~770-805) does `clearPendingBuffer()` BEFORE `uploadPart(remaining)`. The tail is un-durabled while its PUT is in flight; an SW eviction there → respawn completes with only earlier parts → "complete" + share link + silently truncated ending. NOT the accepted R3: that slice was never durable, this one was on disk and the code deleted it.
- **F3 MAJOR** — `resumeInterruptedFinishing` is the only retry-queue writer not serialized through `enqueueRecorder` (`sw.ts:1478`, `onStartup`), racing the alarm drain that fires at the same moment. Lost update on `capExtRetryQueue` → every byte in R2, multipart never completed, state = error promising a background completion that no longer exists.
- **F4 MAJOR** — `sendToOffscreen` broadcasts to every context; an idle recorder tab answers `STOP_CAPTURE` with an immediate `RECORDER_STOPPED` (`recorder-page.ts` ~736, ~881), beating the offscreen doc's async final flush → late chunks silently dropped on EVERY offscreen-path stop while any idle recorder tab exists. The twin already got this right (`offscreen/recorder.ts:285-287` no-ops); only the page misbehaves.

**⚠️ FOUND, NOT YET FIXED — do not lose these:**
- **F5 MAJOR (CONTENT)** — **"Record Meet tab only" is a guarantee-free twin.** Offered on every Meet tab (`popup.ts:258-273`) but has NONE of the guaranteed path's machinery: if audio+video `getUserMedia` fails it silently falls back to **video-only** (`offscreen/recorder.ts:117-128`) and reports full success. No liveness gate, no muted/ended watchdog, no mic-only banner, no confirm modal. A Chrome tab-mute mid-recording is warned about on the full-screen path and invisible here. This is the same class of defect the owner hit today, in the other path.
- **M1** — in "uploading", the popup's only offered action is "Cancel upload" (`popup.ts:716-719`) and `CANCEL` unconditionally sets idle (`sw.ts:791`). During a stuck-offline upload whose bytes are all durable (P1.6 parks state at "uploading" for up to ~2 h), the user's only visible escape destroys the finalize, then burns ~400 attempts and ends in a FALSE "recording is lost" notification. `abortUpload` also never clears that upload's queue items nor aborts the server multipart → parts accumulate in R2.
- **M2** — every browser restart while stuck "uploading" enqueues another duplicate snapshot-less "complete" (`sw.ts:1460-1467`, no dedupe); each fails for ~28 h and dead-letters with a false "lost" notification.
- **M3** — `MEET_NUDGE_CAPTURE_READY` / `contentRecorderTabId` is dead code.

**✅ WHERE CONFIDENCE IS REAL — Fable tried and COULD NOT break:** the part list (no gap / duplicate / out-of-order / unequal non-final part can reach `completeMultipart` — `takeFixedSizeParts` + M7 numbering + R4 dead-letter guard + P1.6 deferral + server `isSequentialPartList`); stale-snapshot clobbers in the live pipeline (R2/R7 identity guards hold, every writer except F3 goes through `enqueueRecorder`); crash mid-completeMultipart (R9 + H3 hold, and H3's headObject cannot false-positive since each recording gets a fresh videoId → fresh fileKey); stuck-forever states (R8, tab-closed, Meet-ended, part-exhaustion all resolve); the `0327eea` URL-param meeting guarantee itself; false "too short" (H1). **The engine is strong — the failures are all at the entry/exit seams.**

**📱 NEEDS A REAL DEVICE (no test can prove these):** F4 — leave an idle recorder tab open, record tab-only 30 s, stop, compare video duration to the timer. F5 — Chrome-mute the Meet tab mid-recording, check the file has silent client audio with zero warning. F2 — throttle to ~1 Mbps, stop, kill Chrome during "Finishing", relaunch, check the ending. Plus mp4/h264 200 ms-slice concatenation playability on the team's actual Chrome version.

## ⭐ OWNER PRODUCT DECISIONS (2026-07-18, SESSION i) — BINDING, do NOT re-litigate

Pinned during the Codex production-readiness review. These define the access/billing model:

1. **THREE KINDS OF PERSON (owner confirmed "ha shunday", 2026-07-18).** This is the access model — build everything against it:
   - **Guest** — no account. Arrives via a share link. Can WATCH + use AI CHAT (daily-capped). Nothing else.
   - **Requester** — signed up on their own. Has an account but **NO organization membership at all**. Sees a friendly "we'll contact you to open your workspace" screen, NOT a dead end — a curious customer signing up is a **sales lead**, so the owner gets a list of pending requests in the dashboard (who, when, which email) with approve/dismiss.
   - **Member** — the owner explicitly added them to the company org. Full product.
   **Free users never record.** They only watch + chat. So a self-signup needs NO workspace of its own — the earlier "give each signup their own org" plan is CANCELLED (owner: storage is only 10 GB free; signup exists mainly so the owner can add members).
   **Signup stays OPEN and needs NO email verification** — safe precisely because a stranger who signs up sees nothing and stores nothing. The C2 defect is that `apps/web/actions/auth/signup.ts:135` calls `getDefaultOrganizationId()` and inserts `organizationMembers role:"member"` → today every internet stranger becomes a member of the owner's team org. Fix = create the user with NO org membership.
   Telegram notification on a new request: raised, **not confirmed by the owner** — do not build it until asked.
2. **AI spend is privileged.** Only **admin/owner** can trigger paid AI *analysis* (transcript, summary, generation). A free user cannot spend AI money.
3. **AI chat is available to free users AND to anonymous visitors arriving via a share link** — but **rate-limited (owner chose option B)**: a per-video / per-client daily cap so guest chat cannot drain the Gemini budget. Not unlimited (C rejected), not admin-only (A rejected). Rationale: guests seeing the product is a growth channel, the cap keeps the bill bounded.
   **ALREADY BUILT** — `apps/web/lib/ai-chat-limit.ts` `DAILY_CHAT_LIMIT = 20`, DB-backed (`ai_chat_usage` table, unique on videoId+clientId+dateUtc, migration `0036`), so it survives restarts. Keyed by userId when signed in, else a cookie, else `guest_ip:<ip>`. Kept at **20** (status quo, live-verified); owner never asked to lower it to 10 — do not change the number without asking.
   ⚠️ **KNOWN BYPASS, not yet fixed:** the IP is read as `x-forwarded-for.split(",")[0]` — the LEFTMOST hop, which a caller can spoof. Repo-wide convention (`password.ts`, `helpers.ts`, `api/utils.ts` do the same). Fixing it means taking the proxy-appended hop instead, but **Coolify's proxy behaviour is unverified and is NOT in this repo** — must be tested live against data365.duckdns.org before changing. Do not guess.

5. **AI spend gate — a hole was found and closed this session.** `apps/web/app/api/videos/[videoId]/retry-ai/route.ts` had NO `canUseAI` check: any signed-in video owner could POST to it and trigger paid Gemini generation. It has zero callers in the web UI (orphaned duplicate of `generate` with `reprocess:true`) but was a live reachable route. Now gated with the same `canUseAI` + `AI_ACCESS_DENIED_CODE` as generate/continue-paid/translate. Note `canUseAI` = global `users.isAdmin` ONLY; org-level "owner" role grants no AI spend (verified: all 14 `role === "owner"` sites are org-scoped and cannot escalate to platform admin).
4. Domain purchase is still open; email sending is NOT configured (`.env` has no mail provider) and decision 1 means it is not on the critical path.

## ▶▶ /CLEAR HANDOFF (2026-07-18 — SESSION h) — FABLE **+ CODEX** RE-AUDITED THE HEART; 6 MORE SAVE-LOSS BUGS FIXED. READ FIRST.

🎯 **GOAL (unchanged):** production-ready for the IT team. #1 = **a meeting must NEVER fail to record.**

**WHAT HAPPENED:** owner called meeting-record "the heart of the project" and asked for BOTH Fable and Codex to review it. Both ran independently against HEAD (after R1/R2/R4) and **both returned STILL BROKEN** — converging on the same defects, which is why they were trusted and fixed. All six below are committed on `qa-fixes`, each with a regression test **proven RED first**.

- **R6 `a640bff` (CRITICAL, both models):** the flat `MAX_ATTEMPTS = 6` + 256s backoff gave every queued item a **~10-minute death clock**. A 10-min WiFi drop mid-meeting dead-lettered a part (R4 then correctly blocks completion → whole 2h meeting lost); a 10-min server outage after a meeting dead-lettered the `complete` item, declaring the recording lost **while every byte sat safely in R2**. Now kind-aware: `PART_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 30` (~1.9h), `FINALIZE_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 400` (~28h), both still bounded.
- **R7 `becee42` (MAJOR, both):** `handleChunk`'s two post-PUT continuations checked only `kind === "recording"`, not videoId/uploadId. CANCEL and RECORDER_STARTED write state outside `enqueueRecorder`, so cancel-mid-PUT + start-a-new-meeting wrote the OLD upload's part into the NEW recording → foreign/non-sequential part list that can never complete = new meeting lost. Added the identity guard `recordCompletedPart` already had.
- **R8 `6d7dc3b` (MAJOR, both):** `sendControlWithRetry` swallowed total delivery failure, so STOP / Meet-ended answered `{ok:true}` while the recorder never stopped. With a dead offscreen doc nothing ever emits `RECORDER_STOPPED` → stuck in "recording" forever, uploaded parts never completed, and CANCEL (the user's next move) destroys them. Now returns a delivered flag and falls back to `finalizeRecording()`.
- **R9 `62bcffa` (CRITICAL, Codex):** `FinishingState` persisted only a videoId, so a crash during completeMultipart destroyed the data needed to retry — startup could only say "may have completed; otherwise re-record". Now carries uploadId + parts and `resumeInterruptedFinishing()` re-queues the finalize on startup (UI still released so new recordings work; drain flips it to "complete" with the share link).
- **R10 `748d2f6` (CRITICAL, Codex):** a `chrome.storage.local.set()` throw (quota/IO) unwound `handleChunk` **after** the full parts were sliced out of the buffer — never uploaded, never queued = silent 5 MiB mid-video hole (R2 accepts gaps). The persist is now non-fatal.
- **R11 `963772c` (owner-raised, web recorder):** crossing the 128 MiB in-flight cap threw a fatal "Upload could not keep up" and destroyed an otherwise-healthy recording on a slow uplink. Now backpressure (bytes stay buffered, finalize loops); only a >512 MiB total backlog still fails loudly.

**🧪 GATES:** extension **27** tests (was 19) · web **906** · web tsc **0** · extension tsc: no new errors (pre-existing `recorder-page.ts`/`sw.ts` cast debt — the extension is NOT tsc-gated in CI, noted in RUNBOOK §7).

**🔍 TWIN CHECK (done, clean):** the web recorder (`instant-mp4-uploader.ts`) does NOT share R1/R2/R4 — different architecture (in-page, single `parts` array, sorted complete, fail-fast). Its equal-size invariant is correct (fixed 16 MiB parts, smallest part always highest partNumber). Web recorder = instruction videos; **meetings = the extension.**

**⚖️ ACCEPTED, NOT FIXED (owner decisions — do NOT re-raise):** R3 crash-mid-upload loses the in-flight ~5 MiB slice (needs a process crash at the exact instant); R5 ~48 GiB / ~24–50 h single-recording cap. Both documented in RUNBOOK §7.

**✅ ALSO CONFIRMED SAFE by both models:** the equal-size/length guarantee itself (`takeFixedSizeParts` — neither could break it, so a **2-hour meeting is fine**); ETag `RESOLVE_SERVER_SIDE` really is resolved server-side via `listParts`; the developer-SDK `/complete` is unreachable for extension videos; `inMemoryBuffer` stays < 5 MiB.

**🎨 UI (owner ask, same session) — `197c41f`:** the transcript-language picker (uz/ru/en) was an ugly 3-dot overlay floating on the video (`absolute right-3 top-3`). Owner chose to move it into the **player's settings (gear) menu**; it is now a submenu there beside playback speed/quality — same items, labels, i18n keys and behaviour. The overlay component is deleted, `LANGUAGE_LABELS`/`ALL_LANGUAGES`/`TranscriptLanguageOptions` are exported for the menu, the prop is threaded ShareVideo → CapVideoPlayer → MediaPlayerSettings, and the now-dead docked-chrome.css rule that hid the old overlay was removed. The actively-recording placeholder deliberately does NOT get the picker (no transcript yet). Built by a Sonnet subagent to a tight spec; **I reviewed the diff and re-ran the gates myself** (its "tsc 0" claim was checked, not trusted). ⚠️ **VISUAL confirm still owed** — fold it into the owner's device walk: open a share page → gear menu → the language submenu appears, switching still works, and nothing overlaps while the mini-player is docked.

**🔨 DEPLOY-READY, NOT DEPLOYED:** `dist/` + `apps/web/public/365-extension.zip` rebuilt with ALL extension fixes (verified in both bundle and zip). **prod is still `f2e64a6`; qa-fixes HEAD = `197c41f`.** Gates at handoff: web tsc **0** · web **906** · extension **27**.

## ▶▶ /CLEAR HANDOFF (2026-07-18 — SESSION g) — FABLE AUDITED "RECORDING NEVER LOST" → BROKEN → 3 SAVE-LOSS BUGS FIXED. READ FIRST.

🎯 **GOAL (unchanged, owner-set):** production-ready for the owner's IT team. The #1 thing "ready" MEANS: **a meeting must NEVER fail to record.** Rank everything against that.

**WHAT HAPPENED:** owner asked Fable to adversarially verify (try to REFUTE) the guarantee "a recording can never fail to save due to length / the R2 equal-size-part rule / similar." **Verdict: BROKEN** — the core `takeFixedSizeParts` math is correct, but 4 other save-loss paths exist. Fixed the 3 that matter; owner accepted the other 2 as documented limits.

**✅ FIXED + COMMITTED on `qa-fixes` (each RED-first tested; extension suite 19→22 green; tsc: my files clean):**
- **R1 `ac9109a` (CRITICAL):** the finalize-time remainder uploaded to R2 but was never persisted to durable state; when a sibling part was still queued (P1.6 path), the deferred `complete` rebuilt the part list from live `state.parts` — missing the remainder — and completed the multipart **without it → video tail silently truncated behind a ✓**. Fix: `recordCompletedPart` the remainder right after its PUT.
- **R2 `8d0b836` (CRITICAL):** PAUSE/RESUME (`sw.ts:575-653`) read state → awaited `sendControlWithRetry` (multi-hundred-ms) → wrote back the **stale snapshot**, clobbering a part `handleChunk` recorded during that window (rewound `nextPartNumber` → next part reused the number, **overwriting 5 MiB in R2**). Fix: route only the state write through `enqueueRecorder` + re-read fresh; control message stays outside so pause is still instant. (Confirms the STATE-flagged risk.)
- **R4 `5ca4281` (MAJOR):** a dead-lettered part left the queue, so the `complete`'s sibling-in-queue guard no longer saw it → completeMultipart ran on the gapped list (**5 MiB hole**) and `stillOurs` flipped error→"complete" with a share link, contradicting the "lost" notification. Fix: before completing, check the dead-letter store for a part of this upload; if found, drop the complete (honest error already set).

**⚖️ OWNER DECISIONS THIS SESSION (do NOT re-litigate):**
- **R3 (browser CRASH mid-upload loses the ~5 MiB in-flight slice → a few-second mid-recording hole)** → **DOCUMENTED, not fixed.** Rare (needs a process crash at the exact upload instant; normal close/stop/network are handled); full fix = persist every slice to disk before upload = constant churn. In RUNBOOK §7.
- **R5 (single recording > ~48 GiB / ~24–50 h fails — the 10,000-part S3/R2 cap at 5 MiB/part)** → **DOCUMENTED, not fixed.** No meeting reaches it. In RUNBOOK §7.
- **Railway retired copy → DELETED** by owner (was live at `capweb-production-dd85...`, now `404`; leaked-credential DB exposure closed).

**✅ ALSO DONE:** `recover-cron.yml` on `main` fixed (`2c8ccef`, pushed) — it pinged the dead Railway URL and never touched real prod; now targets `data365.duckdns.org` with health-check + poll-batch-jobs. ## ▶▶ /CLEAR HANDOFF (2026-07-17 — SESSION f) — FOUND + FIXED A LIVE RECORDING-LOSS BUG. READ FIRST.

🎯 **GOAL (owner re-baselined this session):** production-ready for the IT team — and the #1 thing "ready" MEANS: **a meeting must NEVER fail to record.** Rank everything against that.

**🔴 THE HEADLINE: extension recordings longer than ~2-3 min have been FAILING IN PRODUCTION, and still are for the team's installed build.**
R2 requires *"all parts except the last must be the same size"*. `apps/browser-extension/.../upload.ts` flushed the WHOLE buffer whenever it crossed 5 MiB → every part a different size → `completeMultipart` → `InvalidPart` → recording lost, bytes stranded as an unfinished multipart upload. **`2a405a5` fixed exactly this in the WEB uploader and missed the extension twin** (the two packages share no code, so no caller/reference search links them). This is the real cause of the prod `InvalidPart` errors the SESSION-e audit waved off as "likely stale logs" — they were live.
- ✅ **FIXED + committed `3582cb9` on qa-fixes** (pure `takeFixedSizeParts` + `handleChunk` uploads only full parts, keeps + PERSISTS the remainder — clearing it would have punched a hole in the middle of a recording on MV3 eviction; I caught that regression reviewing Sonnet's diff).
- ⚠️ **NOT DEPLOYED, NOT re-zipped.** The team's `365-extension.zip` still has the bug. Ships with the v0.3.2 device-test gate below.

**🧷 TRAPS INSTALLED (owner asked for "make these not repeat" BEFORE the fix — a nudge wasn't enough; both relevant rules already existed as hooks and both failed):**
1. **Extension vitest suite** (the package had ZERO tests) asserting the R2 invariant + byte-integrity, incl. an integration test driving the real `handleChunk`. **Teeth verified**: restoring the old logic turns 4/5 red (`expected 5451930 to be 5242880`).
2. **CI now runs the extension suite** — it only ran `@cap/web`, so a test added here would never have executed.
3. **CI guard: any workflow with `schedule:` must exist on the default branch** (`ci.yml`, job `guards`).
4. **CI split into 3 independent jobs** (`test` / `lint` / `guards`) — it was ONE job, so the perpetually-red Biome step SKIPPED everything after it. My schedule guard was originally placed after Biome and would have been dead on arrival.

- `51988d8` **five extension recording-loss paths closed** — crashed-recorder salvage; browser-restart recovery (it used to notify "uploading..." then never finalize, parking state at `"uploading"` forever so `isInProgress` refused every future recording = silent loss + permanent brick); a retry queue that only ran once per browser restart; a dead-letter notification that claimed a local fallback that does not exist; and a failed retry-drain that never enqueued a `complete`, stranding fully-uploaded meetings forever.
- `26e76bc` **web recorder salvage (P1.3)** — a mid-recording MediaRecorder error was a silent no-op: chunks discarded or the multipart left un-finalized, timer still ticking. Plus `/complete` now 400s on a gapped part list, `getFolderVideoIds` got its missing org guard (was an ID-enumeration leak), and transcript text is delimited in every Gemini prompt.
- **Extension tests 0 → 19** (the package had none), **web 895 → 905**. Every one proven RED first — including by deliberately re-breaking the code.
- ⚖️ **A design call I made, deliberately** (Fable audited and agreed): a **successful** salvage reports `"completed"` with the share link, not `"error"`. A red failure screen for a recording that is actually saved makes the user re-record and orphans the cap — the same harm as losing it. The early ending lives in a warning toast.
- 🩹 **Two loss paths that MY OWN fixes introduced, caught by Fable's verify pass and fixed** (`upload.ts`): the every-minute drain could stomp a *newer* live recording's state to `"complete"` (new meeting lost behind a ✓ badge); and dead-letter exhaustion left state at `"uploading"`, so the "please re-record" notification pointed at something the extension then refused. Both now guarded on live-state ownership + tested.

**🕳️ KNOWN-OPEN, NAMED NOT HIDDEN (from the Fable verify pass — do not let these quietly disappear):**
- **`PAUSE`/`RESUME` (`sw.ts:575-653`) mutate live recording state OUTSIDE `enqueueRecorder`** — a part completing during the multi-hundred-ms `sendControlWithRetry` window is rolled back: uploaded to R2 but dropped from `state.parts`, `nextPartNumber` rewound so the remainder reuses its number. **Pre-existing, real, untested. Next pass: enqueue them, with a test.** (I had claimed the "no mutation escapes enqueueRecorder" invariant held; that claim was false and Fable caught it.)
- **A THIRD un-guarded `/complete`** in the developer SDK (`app/api/developer/sdk/v1/[...route]/upload.ts:273`) — no sequential check. Deliberately not fixed: S3/R2 legitimately allow sparse part numbers, so a blind 400 could break an external client, and there are zero in-repo clients to check against. Needs its own small pass.
- Popup `RETRY` (`sw.ts:1062-1069`) discards the pending buffer + retry items.

**⛔ OWNER TODO — the recording-DELETE decision (folded from the Fable plan review; this is a WHAT decision only you can make):**
Web "delete" today is **soft-only** — it hides the cap but never erases the R2 file. Wiring a real purge is on the audit's list, but building it now would create this system's **first unrecoverable delete** in a product with no backup and no versioning. I have deliberately NOT built it. **Choose one:** (a) leave delete soft (safe, but R2 storage grows forever and a client asking "erase my recording" cannot truly be honoured); (b) soft-delete + purge after a grace period (e.g. 30 days) — the recommended middle; (c) immediate hard purge (do this only with a backup in place).

Then on a REAL Meet: (1) no client audio → "Baribir boshlaymizmi?" confirm appears → Boshla → records; (2) mute the Meet tab → banner + recording CONTINUES → unmute → banner clears; (3) close the Meet tab → "reopen" banner + recording does NOT stop; (4) one-click whole-screen; (5) finish → the cap appears in the dashboard and plays (this last step also confirms the prod S3 `InvalidPart` upload errors really stopped — the fix shipped in `f2e64a6`). **This is the release gate: nothing deploys to the team until it passes.** The committed `365-extension.zip` is already 0.3.2 and must NOT reach the team before it.

**🔴 OWNER-ONLY floors (no code/agent can clear — the real path to ready):**
1. ~~**Rotate the live admin password**~~ — ✅ **DONE 2026-07-16** (owner reset it himself via `/dashboard/admin/access` → own row → Reset; that path also bumps `authSessionVersion` = all old sessions killed). Note: the old value remains in git HISTORY forever — that is now harmless, but never paste a credential into a doc again.
2. **Free-tier Gemini** — ⚖️ **OWNER DECISION 2026-07-16: KEEP IT. Risk knowingly accepted; do NOT re-raise.** He was shown Google's own terms twice (free tier = content used to improve Google products + "human reviewers may read, annotate and process your API input and output"; paid tier = not used for training) and chose to keep `GEMINI_API_KEY_FREE` in the live Coolify env. Consequence: meeting audio (incl. any 3rd-party Meet guests) flows to that tier. **Mitigation that MUST now ship:** the record-time consent notice (FIXABLE #4) — it is the remaining cheap defence and is no longer optional polish. If he ever asks to remove the key: it is one env delete, fallback to paid is a clean `if (freeKey)` skip (`generate-ai.ts:906`, `transcribe.ts:753`) — but "Arzon" mode then costs money, since that mode exists for the free tier.
3. ~~**Recording backup** — enable R2 versioning+object-lock~~ — ❌ **THIS ITEM WAS WRONG; the audit was wrong. Corrected 2026-07-16 against Cloudflare's own docs — do not act on the old wording.**
   - **R2 has NO object versioning** (`PutBucketVersioning`/`GetBucketVersioning` are listed UNIMPLEMENTED: https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/api/s3/api/). No "restore previous version" exists. **R2 has no per-object Object Lock either.**
   - What R2 *does* have is **bucket locks** (https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/buckets/bucket-locks/) — and they are the **wrong tool for us, twice over**: (a) they block **overwrite** as well as delete, and our pipeline overwrites constantly — `transcribe.ts:1682` rewrites `transcription.vtt` on every (re)transcribe (uncaught, inside a `"use step"` → retries forever against a permanent 403), `poll-batch-jobs/route.ts:157` and `desktop-segments-recovery.ts:186` (15-min cron) rewrite in place, plus `replace-with-audio.ts:94` and presigned-PUT upload retries. Prefix-scoping can't save us: keys are `{userId}/{videoId}/…`, so there is no common "recordings-only" prefix to lock. (b) The lock is **reversible by any R2-config token** (governance-mode equivalent, no compliance mode) → it does **not** stop the stolen-credential case at all.
   - Also relevant: **web delete is soft-only** (`api/video/delete/route.ts:30` → `Videos.delete` → `repo.softDelete`, no S3 call) and `Videos.permanentlyDelete` genuinely has **0 callers**. Only the **desktop** endpoint hard-deletes bytes.
   - **If reopened, the only real answer is an off-site second copy** (rclone is the documented path; R2 egress is FREE, so cost = destination storage + Class B GETs @ $0.36/M). Superseded for now by the owner decision above.
4. **Device/flow tests** (headless can't): real record→upload→process (confirm the S3 `InvalidPart` prod errors STOPPED — the fix is already in `f2e64a6`, likely stale logs); extension v0.3.2 real-Meet; guaranteed-audio real 2-person Meet; dashboard phone-walk.
5. VPS **reboot** (staged security update); **firewall** Coolify admin ports 8000/8080/6001/6002; **domain** move off `*.duckdns.org` (Chrome "Dangerous site").

**🔧 FIXABLE-BY-ME (code/config — not started; owner OK before I touch main/prod):**
1. **Down-alert** — MY MISS: `.github/workflows/uptime-alert.yml` is on `qa-fixes`, not `main` → scheduled workflows only fire from the DEFAULT branch → it NEVER runs. Move it to `main` (needs a push to main). + owner sets the 2 Telegram secrets.

3. **Deletion/erasure** (web) — wire `Videos.permanentlyDelete` (0 web callers today) to a purge so delete actually erases R2 + publish a retention period. (Also fixes unbounded R2 cost growth. Note: the desktop-app endpoint already hard-deletes; the web/extension surface doesn't.)

## CODEX GUARANTEED CLIENT-AUDIO AUDIT (2026-07-14) — ARCHITECTURE CORRECT, REAL MEET TEST REQUIRED

- Reviewed extension v0.3.0 end-to-end: active Meet popup -> `START_INSTRUCTION` with `sourceTabId + meeting:true` -> screen `getDisplayMedia` -> Meet `tabCapture.getMediaStreamId({targetTabId, consumerTabId})` -> recorder-tab `getUserMedia(chromeMediaSource:"tab")` -> Web Audio mix of Meet audio + admin mic -> MediaRecorder.
- Verdict: Claude's solution correctly avoids dependence on the optional `Share system audio` checkbox. Chrome cannot force that checkbox; the extension separately captures the Meet tab audio. Official Chrome docs confirm the `consumerTabId` flow and the need to route captured audio back to speakers, both implemented here.
- Fail-closed is present: if required Meet tab audio cannot be obtained/live, recording does not start. Mid-recording tab close/navigation/track loss stops and saves the partial recording with a notification.
- Important boundary: the guarantee is enabled only when the extension is opened while the real Meet tab is active and the user chooses **Record full-screen meeting**. Generic **Record screen** from a non-Meet tab is not guaranteed and may need system audio.
- Fresh extension build exited 0; built `dist/background.js` contains `consumerTabId`, `REQUEST_TAB_AUDIO`, and loss-watchdog code. Local zip and live `https://data365.duckdns.org/365-extension.zip` are both v0.3.0; live `/api/health` reports DB/storage ok.
- Honest remaining proof gap: the prototype passed with a YouTube source, not a real two-person Meet. Owner must reinstall v0.3.0 and run the real Meet no-system-audio test before calling this guaranteed in production.

- **🔬 RESEARCH (desktopCapture/system audio, 2026-07-14):** on Windows the "Share audio" checkbox is UNCHECKED by default and NO API can force it → "auto system audio" on entire-screen is IMPOSSIBLE (browser security). So "entire-screen video (desktopCapture→offscreen) + tab audio + mix" is DEAD. Screen video via getDisplayMedia/desktopCapture must live in a real TAB (recorder.html), not offscreen. Validates STATE's prototype-first mandate.

> ## 🗄️ ИSH 1B (eski tahlil — tarix uchun) — Buzuq timestamp formati
> Owner bir video transkriptida buzuq xom VTT ko'rsatdi. **Aniqlangan ildiz:** timestamp formati buzuq — `00:04:90.000` ASLIDA 4.90s (nuqta o'rniga 2-`:` + spurious `.000`; to'g'risi `00:00:04.900`). `00:02:20.000`=2.20s, shuning uchun 1-cue nol-uzunlik (`2:20-->2:20`). Format = `MM:SS:cc.000` (centisekund 3-pozitsiyada `:` bilan).
> **Ikki ko'rinadigan kasallik, bitta ildiz:** (a) vaqtlar XATO hisoblanadi (`vttTimeToSeconds`/`parseTimestamp` `:90`ни 90s deb o'qiydi) → click-seek+caption noto'g'ri; (b) parser buzuq formatда `WEBVTT` sarlavhasi va `-->` qatorlarini MATN deb chiqaradi (leak) — owner copy chiqishida `[00:00] WEBVTT`, `[00:01] 00:02:20-->00:04:90` ko'rdi.
> **+ `**` gigiyenasi:** ekranда `renderMarkdownBold` qalin qiladi (yaxshi), lekin COPY (`cuesToPlainText` TranscriptPanel.tsx:251) + download(SRT) + ehtimol caption literal `**` qoldiradi. Yechim: `**`ни copy/download/caption'da tozalash; ekranда qalin yoki muloyim rang urg'u qoldirish (dizayn: rang=signal).
> **DIQQAT:** bu `qfzytpa3vq20ffd` (toza `00:00:01.923`)dan BOSHQA format — Gemini ba'zi videolarда `MM:SS:cc.000` buzuq formatда chiqaradi (non-deterministik). Kontent o'sha "Loom" uchrashuvi (ehtimol qayta-generatsiya).
> **QILISH (autoresearch, real VTT bilan):** (1) bu videoning real VTT'sini R2'dan olib aniq leak yo'lini repro qilish; (2) ILDIZ: `gemini-transcribe.ts` prompt+post-process'да standart `HH:MM:SS.mmm` majburlash YOKI parserlarga `MM:SS:cc` formatни to'g'ri o'qish + invalid-sekund(>59) + nol-uzunlik guard (`transcript-utils.ts parseVTT`, `TranscriptPanel.tsx parseVTTCues`/`vttTimeToSeconds`, `caption-vtt.ts parseTimestamp` — UCHALA parser); (3) `**` ni copy/download/caption'da strip; (4) parserда WEBVTT/`-->`/timestamp-only qatorlar HECH QACHON matn bo'lmasin (hozir buzuq formatда o'tib ketadi). Matn+xulosa SIFATI yaxshi (owner tasdiqladi) — muammo faqat timestamp+display.

> **DONE — "AI summary/chapters/tasks EMPTY on long videos" bug: root cause FOUND + FIXED (`6a9f8f4`).**
> Root cause: the structured-summary Gemini call (`callAiApi` json=true) was capped at `maxOutputTokens=8192`; a ~65-min meeting needs ~8.6k output tokens → truncated mid-JSON (finishReason MAX_TOKENS) → `JSON.parse` threw → error SWALLOWED → empty stored as COMPLETE. Refined transcript survived via a SEPARATE 65536 call (that's why refined showed but summary didn't). PROVEN by standalone repro (`scratchpad/gemini-repro*.mjs`): 8192 → MAX_TOKENS/0 chapters/0 tasks; 65536 → STOP/30/30.
> Fix (2 files, `generate-ai.ts` + `gemini-transcribe.ts`): (1) flat `maxOutputTokens: 65536` on the shared callAiApi (covers single + multi-chunk synthesis). (2) loud logs on MAX_TOKENS truncation + every JSON parse-fail (parseAiResponse, synthesis, per-chunk) — no more silent swallow. (3) `saveResults` marks COMPLETE only when summary is actually usable (non-empty overview/topics/tasks/chapters/nextSteps); empty/refined-only → ERROR (retryable). (4) `gemini-transcribe.ts` throws on empty transcript → ERROR not empty-COMPLETE. Verified: typecheck PASS + 12-agent adversarial review = SHIP + repo-wide sweep = NO other undersized-cap call site. Lesson saved → [[playbook_tech_gotchas]] (LLM-JSON-truncation).
>
> **AI fix LIVE-VERIFIED ✅** (owner confirmed: chapters/summary/tasks now appear on a real video).
>

Built by 3 Sonnet agents (disjoint files) + reviewed + typecheck PASS. Lesson → [[playbook_tech_gotchas]] native-`<track>` entry.

## ✅ Fixed this session (commit · what · root cause)
- `936a4c3` **Transcription/AI silently failed** — Workflow Dev Kit is DISABLED in `next.config.mjs`, so `await start(xWorkflow,[payload])` threw "invalid workflow function". Converted 6 call sites to inline fire-and-forget `xWorkflow(payload).catch(...)` (video-processing, desktop-segments-finalization, save-edits, retry-processing, loom, reprocess-video).
- `29e58e1` **5-sec video showed another video's transcript / "Transcription failed"** — storage key used `userId` instead of `video.ownerId` in `workflows/transcribe.ts` + `generate-ai.ts`; fixed keys + R2 NotFound catch in `S3BucketAccess.headObject` + a warn in `get-transcript.ts`.
- `29e58e1` **Open signup (owner chose option B) + roles** — new `actions/auth/signup.ts` (bcrypt, role `member` NOT admin, joins default org, dup/race-guarded, optional `CAP_ALLOWED_SIGNUP_DOMAINS`); real signup form. Verified live: form renders.
- `29e58e1` **Hide "No custom domain set"** — `Navbar/Items.tsx` gated on `isDomainSetupVerified`. Verified live.
- `ec99b4d` **Org-switcher labels overlapped nav** — clearance `mt-5→mt-8`. Verified live (+6px gap).
- `0e6dd81` **Mic recorded silent / default off** — `useDevicePreferences.ts` auto-selects the first mic on open; `systemAudioEnabled` defaults true. Clearer extension-reconnect message in `meet-detect.ts`.
- `c63444d` **In-recording mic button did nothing** — real `ActionButton` + `toggleMicMute` (flips `track.enabled`); clearer unzip step on extension install page.
- `57fcc0f` **X couldn't close the recorder mid-recording** — X now MINIMIZES during an active recording (re-open guarded by `wasMinimizedRef` because `resetState` is async).
- `3cbfd5a` **Extension "Sign in first" after signing in** — served `365-extension.zip` was STALE; source was correct. Rebuilt via `node scripts/build.mjs` + `Compress-Archive` (Windows has no `zip`); correct API base URL verified in background.js.
- `53cbb83` **"System audio wasn't captured" scary warning every full-screen record** — Chrome/Windows CAN'T capture system audio on full-screen (OS limit, can't be forced). Now: no-mic AND no-system-audio → real warning (truly silent); mic on but no system audio → calm once-per-session info; otherwise nothing. `useWebRecorder.ts`.
- `d7072c6` + `d093fd7` **Extension sign-in didn't work — TWO root causes, both LIVE-VERIFIED by owner ("ha ishladi"):** (1) Chrome silently DROPS the `https://*.up.railway.app/*` externally_connectable wildcard because `railway.app` is on the **Public Suffix List** → the live callback origin wasn't allowlisted → token handshake blocked. Fix: list the prod origin EXPLICITLY in `manifest.json` + rebuild/re-zip `365-extension.zip`. (2) `/extension/callback` was missing from the `proxy.ts` self-hosted allowlist → the sign-in tab was bounced `/extension/callback → /login → /dashboard/caps` and the page never rendered ("This page couldn't load"). Fix: add `path.startsWith("/extension/")`. **Same bug class as the old embed-allowlist miss.** Both verified live (served zip manifest has the origin; callback now returns 200, not a redirect).
- `19bec93` **Extension forced repeated re-sign-in (401 "User not authenticated" on `createVideo`)** — `mint-key/route.ts` DELETED the user's prior `purpose="extension"` key on every call, so a token already saved in the extension died whenever the sign-in callback re-ran (fragile `sendMessage` hand-off compounded it). Now sign-in ADDS a key (keeps newest 5, never prunes the just-minted one) instead of rotating → **one sign-in lasts**. No schema change. ROOT typecheck green, BOM-clean, pushed `qa-fixes`. Owner does ONE more sign-in post-redeploy (current token already dead), then never again.
- `a8ca3f6` **Permanent sign-in fix — Chrome identity (`launchWebAuthFlow`)** — root cause of the recurring 401: the web→extension token hand-off (externally_connectable + `sendMessage`) failed SILENTLY — the callback showed "connected" but the token never reached the extension, so it kept sending a dead key. Now the service worker runs `chrome.identity.launchWebAuthFlow` on a `START_SIGNIN` message, reads the token from a validated `*.chromiumapp.org` redirect fragment (open-redirect-guarded, fragment never hits a server), and persists it — ZERO dependency on externally_connectable or web→extension messaging, so it works on any deploy URL and for any user. popup+options just message the SW (no popup-close race); `manifest.json` gains the `identity` permission; served `365-extension.zip` rebuilt with forward-slash paths (bsdtar). Web ROOT typecheck green; extension esbuild build OK; deployed `qa-fixes`. Manual key-paste (Options → Save & Connect) remains as a fallback and was the verified interim recovery.
